halogen: NOTE only %.1f GiB of host memory is left after the weights and the KV pool. The lookup table is read from disk through the page cache and is never held, so this is the cache it gets: under about 10 GiB its rows page in from disk on every long prompt, a prefill takes minutes instead of seconds, and the watchdog can read that stall as a wedge. Lower HALOGEN_KV_POOL_POSITIONS (each 262,144 positions is about 7.2 GiB) or HALOGEN_MAX_TOK, or stop other workloads on this host.
halogen: NOTE this GPU's firmware (MEC 0x%lx) predates the 2026 linux-firmware releases and stops the engine on some prompts at the default settings, so the server runs as with HALOGEN_QSA_FORCE_SEL=1, at about the same speed. Updating linux-firmware lifts this; HALOGEN_QSA_FORCE_SEL=0 overrides it.
halogen: NOTE this server now holds most of the machine, and %ld contiguous 2 MiB blocks remain. It runs best on a host of its own. If other large processes share this machine they will compete for what is left, and when it runs out BOTH they and this server can stall for minutes at a time at full CPU with no disk activity and no output, which is not a crash and needs no restart. Lowering HALOGEN_KV_POOL_POSITIONS gives some back.%s
